Also use it when reviewing a skill that shells out to local tools (gh, acli, pi, opencode, railway, psql) and the user mentions cloud. ([marwen-abid/dot-env](https://github.com/marwen-abid/dot-env/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cloud-compat))
- **cloud-compatible-file-output**: Use when after feature clustering and drift correction (Gravity and Blueshift outputs) are complete and you need to produce a final, validated feature table ready for storage, archival, or downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cloud-compatible-file-output) · 2 copies)
- **cloud-compile-phone-install**: Autonomously offloads heavy Android Gradle builds and JVM compilation to GitHub Codespaces (16 GB RAM Azure VM), retrieves the generated APK with zero local memory consumption, and deploys it directly to a physical USB-connected phone via ADB. Trigger on /cloud-install, /build-phone, or whenever user asks to compile, build, test, or install the app on their mobile device. ([meabhisheksoni/skill](https://github.com/meabhisheksoni/skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cloud-compile-phone-install) · ★1)
- **cloud-compile-phone-install**: Autonomously offloads heavy Android Gradle builds and JVM compilation to GitHub Codespaces (16 GB RAM Azure VM), retrieves the generated APK with zero local memory consumption, and deploys it directly to a physical USB-connected phone via ADB. Features full autonomous self-healing: if the Codespace is deleted or expires, it automatically re-provisions a fresh 16GB cloud VM, bootstraps Java 17 & Android SDK 36, and completes the build with zero human intervention. Trigger on /cloud-install, /build-phone, or whenever user asks to compile, build, test, or install the app on their mobile device. ([meabhisheksoni/skill](https://github.com/meabhisheksoni/skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cloud-compile-phone-install/cloud-compile-phone-install) · ★1)
- **cloud-compile-prompt**: Use when a Cloud (BlueFox/Pixal) Create job needs a CompiledPrompt from text and/or refs. Props/hard-surface Car-class only. Emits t2i prompt fields plus i2_3d intent tags. Not for characters, Water BYOK, mesh generate, or scoring. ([HydrillaAI/hydrilla-ai](https://github.com/HydrillaAI/hydrilla-ai/tree/HEAD/apps/frontend/agent-skills/Hydrilla%20AI%20orchestration/skills/cloud/cloud-compile-prompt) · ★3)
- **cloud-compliance-auditor**: Automated cloud compliance auditing across AWS, Azure, and GCP. Performs security assessments, policy compliance checks, and generates remediation recommendations. Supports multiple compliance frameworks including CIS, NIST, PCI-DSS, HIPAA, and SOC2. ([lloydchang/agentic-reconciliation-engine](https://github.com/lloydchang/agentic-reconciliation-engine/tree/HEAD/core/ai/skills/cloud-compliance-auditor) · ★2)
- **cloud-compute-modal**: Serverless heavy compute, device-calibrated noise simulation, OpenQASM 3.0 deserialization, batch grading fidelity calculation, and asynchronous QPU job dispatch via Modal.com and Qiskit 1.2 for the Quantum Algorithm Learning Platform.
